# S7-01 — Site Health and first asset risk

Status: outline; refine after technical gates and owner wave input, before activation
USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true
Milestone: S7 Health
Depends on: S2-02 central diagnostics, S1-02 bindings, bounded readiness/health contract and D-edge-health-cases. S6-02 is ordering history only.
Branch: task/S7-01-site-health
Planner: Codex, 2026-10-07

## Visible outcome

Site health and battery asset detail separate source freshness, observed stress and unknown state.

## Persisted object and read contract

Site source-health read model from cadence/heartbeat, ingestion lag and edge diagnostics; a bounded battery stress/asset-life assessment linked to Asset only when a causal observation supports it, otherwise an explicit Unknown state.

## Demo acceptance

Use centrally received gateway diagnostics from S2-02 for stale contact, queue age/depth, clock offset and disk pressure; a local Lab status alone cannot support Site Health. Planner names `D-edge-health-cases` for outage/pressure and verifies central reload. Show battery stress only if a causal simulator observation exists; otherwise name the missing input, show Unknown and withhold replacement exposure. `I-sim-battery-cases` is outside the S7 queue and captured-device replay is unavailable for this simulated-only demo. Reload the central diagnostic and asset states.

## Missing/conflicting evidence

Absent evidence is Unknown, not healthy; no remaining-life date or failure diagnosis without inputs.

## Evidence lineage

Source-health state resolves central diagnostic IDs and server receive times. Any supported battery claim additionally resolves accepted readings, configuration, assumptions and SourceEnvelope IDs; an Unknown state names the missing input.

## Review and refinement

`I-source-health-read/ui` owns `health:2` from centrally received diagnostics and `D-edge-health-cases`. `I-asset-boundary-read/ui` owns only the Unknown/missing-input core of `asset:3`; positive physical condition remains an unmapped, non-core gap pending accepted causal battery observations.

Reviewer opens the selected-window surface and read API, verifies storage/reload and the state above, and follows lineage. Apply shared gates in the current task queue. Before implementation, refine exact fixture, wire/API fields, migration seam and focused checks using the reviewed predecessor and accepted architecture/technical design. This outline is not permission to change those contracts.

## Defer

Global Assets root, broad health rules and unsupported maintenance next steps.

Codex Architect, resolving a bounded selected-window CapabilityReadiness/SourceHealth contract for all three domains, separate from the still-open broad shared evidence projections; detail in `.agent/architect-pre-S3-answers.md`. CapabilityReadiness uses declared role/rule version, selected Foundation/bindings and **accepted** evidence for a tenant/Site/domain half-open window at `knowledge_at`. It returns per-role coverage, quality, missingness, exact revision/evidence IDs and watermark with `READY`, `LIMITED`, `UNKNOWN` or `NOT_APPLICABLE`; rejected, duplicate, held or pending gateway rows do not prove observed capability. Foundation configuration readiness remains distinct. SourceHealth separates **central contact freshness** based on server receipt and declared cadence from the **age and clock basis of reported facts** (`observed_at` and source-contact fields). A newly received old diagnostic may mean contact CURRENT while reported queue/device facts are old or Unknown. Virtual source time is not compared with real server time as physical freshness or network lag. Neither projection is asset condition; lagging read models state `STALE_PROJECTION`, and both expose source/gateway IDs, times, coverage and lineage. Cold/EV role rules and implementations wait for their waves; S7 outage/pressure fixture `D-edge-health-cases` is not required for ordinary S2 contact.
.ai/DECISIONS.md:4015:**Fixture and reuse boundary.** `I-sim-dispatch-cases` supplies a versioned, repeatable manifest of complete, missing-role/quality, contradictory same-role/time and late/out-of-order public intervals, with expected classification ceiling, selected public cohort, source/configuration revisions and exact batch/evidence IDs. Include the S2 overlapping runs and fault epoch as an ambiguity/exclusion check; prove at least one supported Candidate only after the available-PV/PCS-capability binding and accepted-report gate closes, plus Necessary and Indeterminate cases. Seal and upload every product fixture via the registered simulated gateway, Batch V2 and ordinary authenticated S2 ingestion; rejected siblings remain rejected and private oracle data stays Lab-only. The shared `EvidenceQuery`/`AnalyticInputSet`, run/revision, watermark and claim-limit contracts can be reused by later cold-chain/EV analytics, whose role sets, domain rules, fixtures and UI remain later-wave work.

# A-finding-index - independent review (fresh Claude reviewer)

Date: 2026-10-15
Reviewed: branch `plan/A-finding-index`, HEAD `002f8cfe` ("A-finding-index resolved"), base `789d76d1`.
Inputs: `.agent/architect-A-finding-index-request.md`, `.agent/architect-A-finding-index-answers.md`, `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index` (`.ai/DECISIONS.md`), registry diff, `tasks/S4-04-site-finding-index.md`, `tasks/S5-02-overview-finding-list.md`, related decisions, v69 `sitefindings` mock, S4-01 code on this base, S5-01 read on `task/S5-01-site-overview` (via `git show`).

## Verdict: RETURN (one Medium; fix is a short contract amendment)

The contract is otherwise sound and implementable. One Medium fail-closed gap: the decision never applies the existing S3 performance-cutover source gate to the index rows/zero claim.

## Findings

### M1 (Medium, acceptance/contract gap) - S3 source gate missing from index currentness

- Where: `.agent/architect-A-finding-index-answers.md:18` (currentness) and `:28` (money); `.ai/DECISIONS.md` `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`.
- What: "source-open" appears only as a money gate ("unresolved source-open state means no current amount"). The currentness rules for `CURRENT` (complete discovery, no dirty/leased/failed work, no watermark mismatch) do not mention the Site's S3 performance-cutover gate.
- Why it matters: on this base `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:86-90,104-107,116-117` refuses with 409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED` when the gate is closed: "no current Finding or no-Finding verdict is shown. An exact revision_id reads audit material." The S5-01 read on `task/S5-01-site-overview` (`site_overview/read.py`) does the same: it drops to `STALE_PROJECTION`, chooses no unpinned Finding (`SOURCE_CLOSED_NO_UNPINNED_FINDING`) and allows `ABSENT` only on an open source. As written, an S4-04 implementer can return `CURRENT` positive rows, or a `CURRENT` zero-row "no Findings", for a closed-source Site. That contradicts Finding detail, which would then 409 or show audit-only, and contradicts S5-01 on the same Site/day. S5-02 would then show list rows next to an S5-01 card that says no current Finding.
- Fix: add one rule to the answers file and the decision. A closed S3 source gate (whatever reviewed form it takes, including S4-05 stored provenance once merged) forbids `CURRENT` and any zero-row claim. Either refuse the way the existing Finding routes do (409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED`), or return the rows as audit-only/historical with exact revision links and the gate's reason, and say which. Add a closed-gate variant to the `D-site-finding-index-empty` fixture list (alongside unassessed/dirty/failed) and the matching registry acceptance target.

### L1 (Low) - discovery fence for multi-day and boundary-crossing windows not pinned

- `answers:17-18`: "complete scoped discovery" is not tied to a concrete set of analytic windows. On this base, completeness is per UTC-day analytic window (`postgres_finding_store.match_state`: `analytic_run_selection.seq` vs `finding_match_pass.selection_seq`). An episode that intersects `[from,to)` can be anchored in an earlier day through the 20-minute continuity bridge. The Planner should require every intersecting UTC-day window and its continuity neighbours to be matched through their current selection seq (as LR-01 requeue scope already does) before `CURRENT`. The Planner should also bound the selectable window length, since page size is bounded but window span is not.

### L2 (Low) - "reconcile `findings_over`" could break existing consumers

- `answers:32`: the helper's inclusive end and ascending order are correctly identified as non-conforming (`postgres_finding_store.py:418-430`). It is also consumed by `scheduling/health.py:252`, `scheduling/upstream.py:133` and `analytics/finding_read.py:617`, which S5-01 extends. The S4-04 packet should add a new index read, or prove those consumers unaffected, rather than change the helper's semantics in place.

### L3 (Low) - S5-01 card and index head should provably agree

- Ordering (episode_start desc, Finding UUID asc) matches `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice` and S5-01's `choose()` (greatest start, smallest `str(uuid)`). PostgreSQL uuid byte order equals lowercase-hex string order, so they agree. Recommend that S4-04/S5-02 include a test showing that, for the same basis, the first index row equals the S5-01 card Finding.

### L4 (Low, housekeeping) - stale planning text and anchor style

- `tasks/README.md:9` still says `A-finding-index` "remain[s] open", and `tasks/S4-04-site-finding-index.md:14` still says "Decide `A-finding-index`". The Planner should refresh both at S4-04 refinement.
- The registry evidence uses the lowercase anchor `#d-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`, while sibling nodes use `#D-...`. Cosmetic only, since decisions are not headings.

## What was verified

- Implementability on this base: `finding`/`finding_revision` (append-only, `revision_no`, `episode_start <= episode_end`), `finding_merge` (superseded -> canonical), `finding_match_pass` and `analytic_run_selection` seq exist (`0009_finding_store.py`, `postgres_finding_store.py:400-416`). Canonicalization, latest revision selection, the per-window matcher watermark and the strict half-open overlap can all be built without new architecture. The exact detail URL with `revision_id` already exists (`finding_api.py:110-135`).
- Fail-closed honesty: apart from M1, a zero-row claim requires complete current discovery. PENDING/STALE_PROJECTION/FAILED/UNASSESSED never authorize a count or empty claim. Positive rows come only from persisted immutable revisions, with mock F-108/F-109 excluded. A stale list keeps its selected revision links.
- Stable IDs and ordering: the row key is `(tenant, site, finding_id)`; there is no ordering by money or arrival time; the cursor is bound to the basis and invalidated on change; the provisional priority tuple is withdrawn until a producer exists.
- Consistency: matches `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice` (half-open, canonical-only, no lifecycle/money filter, same tie-break) and `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview` (mini:3 belongs to S4-04/S5-02). LR-01 `money_eligibility` and S4-05 exact-revision provenance are consumed only for the optional amount cell. If S4-04 starts before those merge, the cell stays unavailable. Mixed/unknown source and withheld/pending eligibility show no current amount, consistent with `D-2026-10-15-lr01-source-time-cohort` and `D-2026-10-15-s405-stored-demo-provenance`.
- Mock: the decision correctly excludes the v69 `sitefindings` lens chips (All/Cost/Risks/Opportunities), the "N open" count and the "Manage in Findings" global link, none of which have producers.
- Registry: `node frontend/docs/architecture/check_v69_dependency_registry.cjs` -> "Registry valid: 228 nodes, 11 linked pilot panels", exit 0. `--next-work --json` -> `status: ranked`, not refused, no global stops. It selects `I-finding-index` 2.143 vs `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` 2.021 (5.69% gap, 5-10% tie-break band, `user_review_required: true`, owner notice). Removing `A-finding-index` from `outside_ranking` is correct now that it is resolved, and it stays in `queue_baseline_nodes`, still owned by S4-04. The follow-on nodes `I-finding-index`, `I-site-findings-ui`, `D-site-finding-index-empty/-rows`, `I-S4-04`, `I-overview-finding-list-ui` and `I-S5-02` remain `open`, with acceptance targets consistent with the answers.
- No owner acceptance is claimed: the decision, answers and registry targets all state that the owner walkthrough and copy acceptance remain pending.

Not run: no test suites (out of scope for a no-code architecture review).

# Codex Architect â€” S2 leftovers and pre-S3 contracts (2026-10-09)
### A-claim-work-routes â€” resolve from `D-2026-10-09-pre-s3-claim-routes-and-actor`

- A Finding or Action has an immutable UUID plus tenant and Site UUID. Canonical product routes are `/sites/{site_code}/findings/{finding_id}` and `/sites/{site_code}/actions/{action_id}`; APIs are `/api/sites/{site_code}/findings/{finding_id}` and `/api/sites/{site_code}/actions/{action_id}`. Each root may have child views when built; tabs do not mint new root identity. Site code is resolved **inside** the authenticated tenant, never as global authority. This route contract is domain-neutral for mini-grid, cold-chain and EV.
- A Finding list links the stable root ID and exact immutable `finding_revision_id` where a historical claim is intended. Omitted `revision_id` selects the latest permitted Finding revision at stated `knowledge_at` and returns that selected ID and watermark; supplied `revision_id` freezes it after later revisions. Action state is reduced from immutable ActionEvents, so its exact historical selection is `event_id`/recorded cutoff; Plan and Verification child views use their own `plan_revision_id` and `outcome_id`. Do not invent an ActionRevision row. Every selected ID must belong to the same root/tenant/Site; root ID alone never implies a particular historical statement. Back-links retain Site, window and exact selection.
- Unauthenticated is 401; authenticated but unauthorized Site/root is 403 when that scope is known to the caller, while a foreign-tenant or nonexistent identity is 404 to avoid disclosure. An absent revision is 404; a supported root whose read/projection is not yet available returns a typed unavailable/lagging state, not a fabricated empty successful claim. Unbuilt tabs are hidden or honestly unavailable. Site-scoped lists precede W3 global roots; a future global index points to these same canonical IDs.

### A-actor-identity â€” resolve from the same decision

- Persist `actor_kind` (`HUMAN` or `SYSTEM`), server-resolved `actor_id`, action/source channel, recorded time and authorization basis with each auditable assumption, policy or work event. A human `author_id` is an authenticated principal; a client-supplied string, local OS username, fixed development tenant or gateway credential does not establish a human author. `SYSTEM` events use a stable service principal and the triggering job/command/fixture ID. A system process cannot fill a human author field or imply human attestation.
- Human-attested records require the authenticated human actor plus the exact attested content/version, attested time and immutable proof/reference; an operator's later correction appends a new event/revision with reason. An automated observation, Lab run or gateway receipt is source provenance, not a human sign-off. The S2 provisioning CLI's OS-user name remains labelled **local context**, never verified tenant actor.
- For S4-02's price edit and S6-01's accepted Action/policy change, the Planner must supply a verified operator path or a clearly labelled server-authorized `SYSTEM` demo operation with immutable audit provenance. If neither exists, the write is unavailable; do not forge `financial_assumption_revision.author_id` or let the browser choose it. This is an implementation gate of those packets, not an S3-01 dispatch requirement.

### Bounded A-evidence-readiness-health â€” resolve from `D-2026-10-09-pre-s3-evidence-readiness-health`

- `CapabilityReadiness` is a backend selected-window read of rule-versioned required roles against registered bindings and **accepted** evidence. It carries tenant/Site/domain, half-open window, `knowledge_at`, Foundation/binding/rule revisions, per-role observed coverage/quality/limits, evidence IDs, source/projection watermarks and `READY | LIMITED | UNKNOWN | NOT_APPLICABLE`. Missing required evidence is Unknown or Limited with a reason, never healthy or zero. Rejections/duplicates and pending gateway rows do not satisfy role coverage. Configuration readiness is separately labelled and never substitutes for observed capability.
- `SourceHealth` is an operational projection with at least two distinct clocks: **server contact freshness** from latest centrally received diagnostic (`CURRENT | STALE | UNKNOWN` under registered cadence) and **reported-fact age** from that diagnostic's `observed_at`/source contact times, with clock basis, trust/offset and delayed-report limits. A newly received old diagnostic can make contact CURRENT while its queue/pressure/device-contact facts remain old or Unknown; UI must show both. For simulated `RUN_VIRTUAL` observations, do not compare virtual time with real server time as a physical freshness or network-lag measure. SourceHealth is not asset condition or an AcceptedEvidence row.
- Both reads disclose source/gateway IDs, received/observed times, coverage/missingness, `built_at`, source watermark and projection lag. A stale projection is `STALE_PROJECTION`, not a current capability conclusion. Site read contracts are defined now for all three domains; role requirements and actual cold/EV projections wait for their domain waves. `D-edge-health-cases` adds S7 outage/pressure fixture variants and is not an S2 ordinary-contact prerequisite.

### FindingIndex and money boundary â€” constrained, still open

FindingIndex's eventual row key is `(tenant_id, site_id, finding_id)` with `domain`, stable episode/assessment anchor, selected `finding_revision_id`, lifecycle, window, source/analytic watermark and a stable `(priority_tuple, updated_at, finding_id)` cursor. A Site query includes Site and window; a global query also needs cross-Site scope and comparability. The current pre-S3 decision fixes these keys and lineage, **not** `A-finding-index` resolution: episode and revision production waits for S3/S4, and W3 aggregation waits for all three domains.

Money remains Site/Finding bounded until a reviewed comparison basis declares currency, price/assumption revision, period, coverage, measurement/model/scenario basis and non-overlap group. No unlike currency/period sum, scenario-as-observed amount, unpriced-as-zero, or inferred recovery. `A-money-comparability` stays open until S4 price/consequence and W3 multi-Site fixtures. These guards constrain W1 output now without activating a W3 rollup.

### Existing architecture-node resolution and exact Planner edits

Resolve `A-S1-bindings` (area `configuration`, `requires: [A-S1-foundation]`) with evidence links to `D-2026-10-08-s1-bindings-roles-and-snapshot` and `D-2026-10-08-s1-foundation-organization`. Resolve `A-S2-evidence` (area `evidence`, `requires: [A-S1-foundation]`) with `D-2026-10-07-s2-edge-split-and-demo-source`, `D-2026-10-08-s2-v3-source-payload-and-period-reference`, `D-2026-10-09-s2-pilot-gateway-upload-credential`, `D-2026-10-09-s2-ingestion-operational-semantics`, `D-2026-10-09-s2-evidence-cutover-and-credential-closeout` and `D-2026-10-09-s2-credential-uncertain-commit-and-evidence-demo`, noting S2-02 is separately accepted/merged. These bounded decisions cover their current node descriptions; broader shared projections stay open.

For the Planner's registry reconciliation: set those two nodes `status: resolved`, attach those decision citations in `evidence`/`decision`; set `A-claim-work-routes` and `A-actor-identity` to `resolved` with `D-2026-10-09-pre-s3-claim-routes-and-actor`. Add `A-evidence-readiness-health` with `kind: architecture`, `area: evidence`, `owner: Architect`, `requires: [A-S2-evidence]`, `status: resolved`, `effort: M` and evidence `D-2026-10-09-pre-s3-evidence-readiness-health`; put it in `queue_baseline_nodes` because W1 S7 owns SourceHealth and W1 Evidence/S5 consume bounded readiness. Keep `A-evidence-shared-projections` open for AcceptedEvidenceIndex/SiteEvidenceMatrix and cross-domain breadth, adding the bounded node to its `requires` for the overlapping readiness/health base. Keep `A-finding-index` and `A-money-comparability` open. Do not add product nodes as technical `requires` or mark any panel Real from these architecture decisions. Reconcile the merged S2 implementation nodes against actual accepted review/owner/merge records, not phase commits.

## S3-01 refinement and technical requires

S3-01 still requires the accepted S2-02 ledger (`I-S2-02`), resolved `A-S1-bindings`/`A-S2-evidence`, a new `A-S3-dispatch` decision, and `I-sim-dispatch-cases` produced through the normal registered gateway/ingestion path. These are technical gates; mandatory shared route/actor/readiness/health decisions above are pre-activation architecture work, **not** dispatch input dependencies. The S3 typed `EvidenceQuery`/`AnalyticInputSet` must bind tenant/Site, domain, half-open window, knowledge cutoff, immutable source IDs, accepted evidence IDs/digests, selected Foundation/binding revisions and role coverage. It must filter source-time/interval overlap and distinguish server receive time from virtual publication time. It cannot read Lab world truth or assume today's mapping was the accepted-as mapping.

Refinement must name a selected run/epoch basis for overlapping simulated same-time reports (including the S2 demo's two runs and its labelled fault-fixture epoch), or return limited/Indeterminate coverage; never sum them as one physical stream. It must compare representative file-backed dispatch claims before the Performance read cutover, include complete/missing/contradictory/late intervals, persist analytic input hash/run/assessment revisions, enqueue/consume the S2 recompute jobs and show late-evidence revision selection. Dispatch classifies Necessary/Candidate/Indeterminate with exact accepted evidence lineage, not SourceHealth `CURRENT`, CapabilityReadiness `READY`, or a gateway contact as proof of dispatch. No S4 FindingIndex/global money implementation is pulled into S3.

S3's checker/registry task `requires` should retain `A-S3-dispatch`, `I-S2-02`, `I-S1-02` and `I-sim-dispatch-cases` (with the latter's architecture and gateway dependencies), and `sequenced_after` may retain S2-02 as true predecessor. Do not add `A-claim-work-routes`, `A-actor-identity`, `A-finding-index`, `A-money-comparability` or global evidence projections as S3 technical prerequisites. Owner S2 closeout and V-01 adoption are recorded; W1 core-panel/demo-value input and V-01 implementation acceptance remain separate product gates under the delivery strategy.

# Architect answer - Site FindingIndex (2026-10-15)

Decision: `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`. Resolve `A-finding-index` for the W1 Site read only. Authority is the owner-absence work order item 9, the reviewed S4-01 episode contract, `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview`, and `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`. This decision defines a reusable row contract; it does not implement an estate/global index, cold-chain or EV analytics, or approve UI copy.

## Scope and row selection

- Require an authenticated tenant, resolved Site UUID/code, domain and selected half-open UTC window `[from,to)` of at most 31 UTC days. Reject longer windows. The W1 producer is mini-grid dispatch. Resolve the Site within the tenant and use the established 401/403/404 identity rules. Do not silently expand to another Site, another domain or the mock's global Findings area. Echo the requested window and display timezone; changing dates makes a new scoped read, not a client-side filter of an old list.
- Start with persisted S4-01 Finding and immutable FindingRevision records, never a UI seed, S3 interval alone, Lab private state or a financial consequence. Select the latest permitted revision of each canonical Site Finding from one consistent S3/S4 basis. A row qualifies only when its selected episode intersects the window (`episode_start < to` and `episode_end > from`); a boundary touch does not qualify. Apply S4 merge canonicalization: one row for the surviving ID, with superseded IDs available as relationship/history links. Extension changes the revision on the same Finding; separated recurrence uses a new Finding ID and recurrence link. Closed and unconfirmed episodes remain eligible historical rows in an intersecting window; the default list is not an implicit open-only filter.
- Row identity is `(tenant_id, site_id, finding_id)` with the stable Finding UUID, canonical ID, exact selected `finding_revision_id`, domain, episode bounds, lifecycle/transition, selected assessment/run/model/cohort and cited accepted-evidence/configuration IDs. Include limitations and exact Site detail URL `/sites/{site_code}/findings/{finding_id}?revision_id={revision_id}`; the API may also return an unpinned canonical URL. The selected revision link must survive reload and later revision creation. A row's source and analytic lineage is the selected immutable revision's lineage, not a recomputation from its title or latest unrelated consequence.

## Ordering and pagination

- Do not infer a priority or confidence rank from the v6.9 illustrative rows. W1 default order is selected `episode_start` descending, then stable Finding UUID ascending. This replaces the provisional `(priority_tuple, updated_at, finding_id)` ordering in the pre-S3 guidance until a reviewed priority producer exists. A future reviewed priority rule may add a versioned `priority_tuple`; until then any priority/lens control without a supported producer is hidden or explicitly unavailable. Never order by mutable amount or arrival time. Return a deterministic total count and opaque cursor bound to tenant, Site, domain, window, selected basis/watermark and last sort key. A changed basis invalidates the cursor and requires a fresh first page; it must not splice different revision sets together. Page size is bounded. S5-02 consumes the same ordered Site rows and does not independently rank or select an S5-01 card Finding.

## Watermark and currentness

- Return a read-level selection token/basis ID, accepted-source watermark, S3 assessment selection and analytic watermark, S4 matcher/selection watermark, model/matcher version, `built_at`, coverage/limitations and a typed status. A transactionally consistent scoped read or atomically published projection is acceptable. `knowledge_at` is the stated selection cutoff; do not promise arbitrary commit-ordered historical replay from the current S3 worker clock. Exact revision IDs are the audit anchors.
- `CURRENT` requires complete scoped discovery: every UTC-day analytic window intersected by `[from,to)`, plus neighbouring windows reachable through the S4-01 20-minute episode-continuity rule, must have S3 selection and canonical S4 matching through each window's current selection seq on the same accepted-evidence/configuration/model basis. No affected dirty, leased or failed S3/S4 work, watermark mismatch or moving selection is allowed. Relevant late evidence or changed configuration/model invalidates affected windows and must rebuild or make them stale. `PENDING` means no usable completed selection yet; `STALE_PROJECTION` retains prior rows/IDs with the reason while a newer basis is unresolved; `FAILED` reports failed work and any prior selected rows as historical. `UNASSESSED` means no completed assessment/discovery basis. None of these statuses authorizes a current count, current empty claim or an inferred Healthy state. A source-coverage limitation may coexist with a current, complete assessment of the available evidence; state the limitation instead of calling the physical world complete.
- Apply the Site's S3 Performance cutover source gate before any unpinned index read. If it is closed, refuse the index with HTTP 409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED` and the gate reason, matching the Finding routes. Return neither `CURRENT` rows nor a zero-row/no-Findings claim. Exact `revision_id` Finding detail remains the separate audit-only route. This rule also consumes the reviewed S4-05 stored provenance form of the gate once merged.
- Keep list freshness separate from each row's assessment claim and from money eligibility. A current list can include an `UNCONFIRMED` Finding or a row with no current amount. A stale list cannot silently replace its selected revision links with newer detail heads.

## Independent fixture proof

- `D-site-finding-index-empty`: persist a tenant/Site/domain/window with an accepted-source and S3/S4 selection watermark, complete assessed discovery, zero canonical intersecting rows and a `CURRENT` zero-row response. Prove a wrong Site/window is excluded and a boundary-touch episode is excluded. Separately exercise unassessed, dirty/stale and failed reads, each visibly Unknown/pending rather than a no-Findings assertion; close the S3 source gate and prove HTTP 409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED` with its reason and no current rows or zero claim. A filtered empty page after a positive unfiltered read is not the zero-row fixture.
- `D-site-finding-index-rows`: use committed accepted evidence and S4-01 revisions to persist at least one intersecting canonical Finding, ideally multiple for order and cursor proof. Verify stable ID, latest selected revision, exact detail/history link after reload, recurrence versus extension and canonical merge behavior, cross-Site isolation, date change, boundary intersection and invalidation after a late revision. Empty and positive fixtures are separate W1 closeouts; both must be reviewed. Do not count the mock's F-108 or F-109 as produced Findings without their own reviewed analytics.

## Money and provenance

- A row's existence, order, lifecycle and links are independent of money. The optional consequence cell may use only the exact selected FindingRevision and compatible selected-window consequence. Consume the shared LR-01 `money_eligibility` state and S4-05 exact accepted-ID publication provenance when those reviewed but currently unmerged branches become the build baseline. `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, mixed/unknown source, stale list or consequence, wrong revision/window, unpriced quantity/price or unresolved source-open state means no current amount. Preserve an old value only behind an explicit historical/audit link. Coherent authored or registered simulation must carry its truthful label and still pass exact-head, currentness and quantity/price gates. Do not aggregate, sum overlapping Findings, infer savings, or turn missing price into zero. If S4-04 starts before those branches merge, leave the consequence cell unavailable and add the reviewed contracts before any current-money presentation or S5-02 integration.

## S4-04 and S5-02 handoff

S4-04 builds the authenticated Site/window index read or equivalent projection, affected-work invalidation, stable cursor and exact revision links, then the Site Findings route/list with selected scope, loading/stale/failed/empty/positive states and keyboard navigation. Add a new index read; do not change `findings_over` in place, since `scheduling/health.py`, `scheduling/upstream.py` and `analytics/finding_read.py` consume its inclusive-end and ascending-order semantics. Its selected revisions must share a consistent matcher/assessment fence; a helper alone is not proof of complete discovery. Compare the supported Site screen at desktop and narrow widths, but do not copy unsupported mock rows, global-list links, Cost/Risks/Opportunities filters, priority/confidence badges or open counts without real producers. The reviewer verifies both fixtures independently, same-basis pagination, tenant isolation, reload and exact drill-down. Test that on the same Site/window/basis the first index row's Finding and revision IDs equal the S5-01 card Finding and selected revision. Owner walkthrough and copy acceptance remain pending until performed.

S5-02 consumes this S4-04 contract on the S5-01 selected Site/window and preserves its own scope, watermark and exact row links. It proves `mini:3#a` honest empty/Unknown and `mini:3#b` positive rows independently. S5-01 may retain a known selected Finding card link but does not close either list variant. S4-03 Evidence/History, `mini:1/2/4` and S3 dispatch do not acquire this index as a prerequisite.

It is a policy-sensitivity experiment, not a causal remedy, saving or verification claim; otherwise show a typed unavailable reason. Ratify `actions/adapters/postgres_action_store.py` as the sole ninth PostgreSQL writer for append-only Action, event, change and Action-owned child rows. The Action adapter opens the apply transaction and locks Action before calling a caller-owned-connection S1 publish that locks Site; the S1 adapter writes only Foundation and binding rows, and an Action-adapter callback writes the change artifact and APPLIED event on that same connection before the outer commit. Rule 8 must admit only this ninth writer's INSERTs to its tables, reject Action-table writes by every other module and reject mutation or DDL in the Action adapter while retaining all eight existing protections. Authorized same-Site failures remain separate durable attempts after rollback. A server-authorized, state-neutral SYSTEM demonstration `OBSERVED` event with fixed `DEMO_REVIEW_NOTE` code may carry an explicit `occurred_at` between ACCEPTED and server `recorded_at`, no more than 30 days earlier, to prove backdated history without changing application state. Accepted IDs inherit immutable provenance, duplicates retain their original accepted ID, and historical unbound IDs stay unknown. Decision: `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`. Under the owner-absence work order item 9, resolve `A-finding-index` for a tenant/Site/domain and selected half-open UTC window of at most 31 days. Complete discovery requires every intersected UTC-day analytic window and neighbours reachable through 20-minute episode continuity matched through each current selection seq. Only complete current scoped discovery under an open S3 Performance cutover gate may state zero Findings; pending, stale, failed and unassessed reads remain visibly Unknown or historical. A closed gate refuses the unpinned index with HTTP 409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED` and its reason, with neither current rows nor a no-Findings claim, as on Finding routes; S4-05 stored provenance joins that gate once merged. A changed basis invalidates the bound page cursor. Mixed/unknown source and withheld/pending eligibility expose no current amount. Decision: `D-2026-10-16-s6-verification`. Target is at least 25% less Candidate segment seconds, clipped to each half-open plan window from selected UTC-day `dispatch-3` revisions under S3 bracket/continuity rules; pin exact day revision IDs in the plan selection binding and outcome. Indeterminate or open-bound time is INCONCLUSIVE. Never add an S3 60-minute selection kind or use runtime, money or Finding count as the target. S6-02 adds public requested/delivered critical-load roles and five-minute Lab publication/readiness support; required coverage includes those roles, dispatch roles, cohort/clock basis, SOC and time-valid reserve. No required gap or material conflict may be hidden. Service unserved energy may not increase; later reserve-breach minutes must be zero and not increase; terminal SOC at a comparable horizon may not fall. A missing guardrail is unknown. On a complete pair, target and every guardrail pass yields `VERIFIED`; target or measured guardrail failure yields `INEFFECTIVE`, with `GUARDRAIL_FAIL` as a reason, not an outcome. Missing/ambiguous coverage, comparability, source provenance or unsettled reassessment yields `INCONCLUSIVE` or pending read state; a known failure remains visible without promoting a verdict while other required facts are unknown. Resolve source per window; a compatible pair has the same coherent source class and registered scenario/profile lineage, distinct run digests and intact links. Only `REGISTERED_SIMULATION` or `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` public evidence may support a labelled demo verdict; mixed/unknown source fails closed, and no real-source positive verdict is claimed before real attestation. Late evidence or changed basis withdraws current finality while S3/S4 reassess; append a new immutable outcome when settled, retaining exact plan, assessment, Finding, accepted-envelope and source IDs and old outcomes. The evaluator job appends `RESOLVED` for a final current `VERIFIED` or `INEFFECTIVE`; only VERIFIED or target-miss-only INEFFECTIVE releases the prior-Action hold. Measured reserve/service `GUARDRAIL_FAIL` keeps the Site/asset/field trial blocked with `PRIOR_TRIAL_GUARDRAIL_FAILED` until a future rollback/review contract. `INCONCLUSIVE`, pending and failed application retain the hold. If reassessment invalidates closure, the evaluator job appends `RESOLUTION_WITHDRAWN` and restores the guard atomically with acceptance; a closure/late-reading overlap permanently blocks further acceptance with a typed reason until a future reviewer contract. A changed covering FoundationRevision for either window makes the result pending, then INCONCLUSIVE without a new comparable plan. S6-02 migrates the `action_event` CHECKs for both events and updates `lifecycle.reduce`/`service.is_unresolved` to read latest event under the trial lock; never rewrite history. The system computes metrics and verdict; a server-authorized, labelled SYSTEM demonstration command may request evaluation or plan freeze, never dictate the result or append resolution events. Two fixed-policy Lab runs and success, guardrail-fail and inconclusive variants travel through normal gateway/ingestion; no causal saving is asserted.     def window(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, day: date) -> dict[str, Any]:
        window = day_window(day)
        query = EvidenceQuery(tenant_id, site_id, DOMAIN, window, OPEN_CUTOFF, roles=DISPATCH_ROLES)

        def basis(result: EvidenceQueryResult) -> tuple[str | None, str]:
            inputs, config_sha = input_basis(result, self.model)
            return (None if inputs.cohort.status == "EMPTY" else inputs.input_sha256), config_sha

        snap = self.store.window_snapshot(query, self.model.ANALYTIC_ID, basis)
        projection = derive_status(snap, self.model.MODEL_VERSION)
        run = snap.run
        revisions = [] if run is None else run["revisions"]
        [window_rev] = _scope(revisions, "WINDOW") or [None]
        intervals = _scope(revisions, "INTERVAL")
        live_keys = {r["assessment_key"] for r in revisions}
        superseded = [k for k in snap.key_heads if k["assessment_key"] not in live_keys]
        evidence_ids = {str(e["evidence_id"]) for e in (run or {}).get("evidence", [])}
        return {
            "domain": DOMAIN, "analytic_id": self.model.ANALYTIC_ID, "model_version": self.model.MODEL_VERSION,

            "stale": projection.status in (STALE, FAILED) and run is not None,
            "current_basis": {"read_at": snap.read_at, "accepted_reading_count": snap.reading_count,
                              "input_sha256": snap.current_input_sha256,
                              "configuration_sha256": snap.current_configuration_sha256},

                "cohort_sha256": run["cohort_sha256"], "cohort": run["cohort"],
                "foundation_revision_ids": run["foundation_revision_ids"],
                "binding_set_revision_ids": run["binding_set_revision_ids"], "built_at": run["completed_at"]},
            "coverage": None if run is None else run["role_coverage"],

def freshness(received_at: datetime | None, cadence_seconds: int, now: datetime) -> str:
    """`CURRENT|STALE|UNKNOWN` from server receive time only."""
    if received_at is None:
        return "UNKNOWN"
    return "CURRENT" if now - received_at <= timedelta(seconds=FRESHNESS_CADENCES * cadence_seconds) else "STALE"


def gateway_health_document(facts: list[GatewayHealthFacts], site_id: UUID, now: datetime) -> dict:
    """The Site gateway-health read. Operational only: no asset or device health claim."""
    gateways = []
    for g in facts:
        latest = g.latest_fields
        contacts = dict(latest["last_device_contact_by_source"]) if latest else {}
        gateways.append({
            "gateway_id": str(g.gateway_id),
            "gateway_external_id": g.gateway_external_id,
            "mode": g.mode,
            "registration": "DISABLED" if g.disabled else "ENABLED",
            "declared_cadence_seconds": g.cadence_seconds,
            "status": freshness(None if g.latest is None else g.latest.received_at, g.cadence_seconds, now),
            "status_basis": "SERVER_RECEIVED_AT",
            "last_server_contact": _z(None if g.latest is None else g.latest.received_at),
            "diagnostic_id": None if g.latest is None else str(g.latest.diagnostic_id),
            "gateway_observed_at": None if latest is None else latest["observed_at"],
            "observed_at_clock": "GATEWAY_REAL_UTC",
            "queue_count": None if latest is None else latest["queue_count"],
            "oldest_queue_age_seconds": None if latest is None else latest["oldest_queue_age_seconds"],
            "disk_pressure": None if latest is None else latest["disk_pressure"],
            "disk_pressure_provenance": (
                None if latest is None else
                "REPORTED_SIMULATED_GATEWAY" if g.mode == "SIMULATED" else "REPORTED_GATEWAY"),
            "clock_offset_ms": None if latest is None else latest["clock_offset_ms"],
            "mapping_error_count": None if latest is None else latest["mapping_error_count"],
            "source_contacts": [
                {
                    "source_id": str(source_id),
                    "source_external_id": external,
                    "registration": "DISABLED" if disabled else "ENABLED",
                    "last_contact": contacts.get(str(source_id)),
                    "contact_provenance": (
                        "OBSERVED_SIMULATED_REPORT" if g.mode == "SIMULATED" else "OBSERVED_REPORT"),
                }
                for source_id, external, disabled in g.sources
            ],
        })
    return {
        "site_id": str(site_id),
        "read_at": _z(now),
        "read_at_clock": "SERVER_REAL_UTC",
        "claim_scope": "GATEWAY_OPERATIONAL_REPORT",
        "gateways": gateways,
    }

    def gateway_health(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID) -> list[GatewayHealthFacts]:
        def work(c: Connection) -> list[GatewayHealthFacts]:
            gateways = c.execute(text(
                "SELECT id, gateway_external_id, mode, disabled_at IS NOT NULL AS disabled, "
                "diagnostic_cadence_seconds FROM gateway WHERE tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s "
                "ORDER BY gateway_external_id"), {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id}).all()
            facts = []
            for g in gateways:
                sources = c.execute(text(
                    "SELECT id, source_external_id, disabled_at IS NOT NULL AS disabled FROM source "
                    "WHERE tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s AND gateway_id = :g ORDER BY source_external_id"),
                    {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id, "g": g.id}).all()
                latest = c.execute(text(
                    self._SELECT + "WHERE tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s AND gateway_id = :g "
                    "ORDER BY received_at DESC, diagnostic_sequence DESC LIMIT 1"),
                    {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id, "g": g.id}).first()
                stored = None if latest is None else self._stored(latest)
                facts.append(GatewayHealthFacts(
                    gateway_id=g.id, gateway_external_id=g.gateway_external_id, mode=g.mode,
                    disabled=g.disabled, cadence_seconds=g.diagnostic_cadence_seconds,
                    sources=tuple((s.id, s.source_external_id, s.disabled) for s in sources),
                    latest=stored,
                    latest_fields=None if stored is None else json.loads(stored.diagnostic_text)))
            return facts

        return self._run(work)

    # --- operator provisioning ---------------------------------------------------------

    def _gateway(self, c: Connection, tenant_slug: str, site_code: str, gateway_external_id: str):
        row = c.execute(text(
            "SELECT g.tenant_id, g.site_id, g.id FROM gateway g "
            "JOIN tenant t ON t.id = g.tenant_id JOIN site s ON s.tenant_id = g.tenant_id AND s.id = g.site_id "
            "WHERE t.slug = :slug AND s.site_code_key = lower(:code) AND g.gateway_external_id = :g "
            "FOR UPDATE OF g"), {"slug": tenant_slug, "code": site_code, "g": gateway_external_id}).first()
        if row is None:
            raise ProvisioningRefused(
                f"gateway {gateway_external_id!r} is not registered to Site {site_code!r} in tenant {tenant_slug!r}")
        return row

    @staticmethod
    def _event(c: Connection, g, kind: str, actor: str, at: datetime, *, source_id=None, selector=None) -> None:
        c.execute(text(
            "INSERT INTO gateway_access_event (id, tenant_id, site_id, gateway_id, source_id, credential_selector, "
            "kind, actor, recorded_at) VALUES (:id, :t, :s, :g, :src, :sel, :kind, :actor, :at)"),
            {"id": uuid4(), "t": g.tenant_id, "s": g.site_id, "g": g.id, "src": source_id, "sel": selector,
             "kind": kind, "actor": actor, "at": at})

+# Architect answer - remaining W1 screen contracts (2026-10-16)
+
+Authority: `.agent/architect-W1-remaining-request.md`; registry `frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json` (`sitework:1#a`, `health:2#a`, `asset:3#a/#b`, `evidence-analysis:2#a/#b`); v6.9 Site Work, Site Health, Asset Health and Evidence Analysis screens; Site-first execution plan; existing decisions named below. These are bounded architecture contracts, not implementation, copy approval or panel delivery. The mock's populated Actions, condition claims and cross-domain lists are illustrative.
+
+## S6-03 - `A-action-index` / `sitework:1#a`
+
+**Source and scope.** Add a read over persisted `action` and `action_event`, authenticated to one tenant and resolved Site. W1 is mini-grid Actions only. Select a half-open UTC creation window `[from,to)` of at most 31 days; `created_at >= from AND created_at < to` is the membership rule, irrespective of later events. Echo scope and display timezone. The stable row key is `(tenant_id, site_id, action_id)`; include Action code, creation time, exact origin Finding and FindingRevision IDs, latest selected event ID, event recorded time, reduced lifecycle, and the canonical and exact-event Action links. The detail/event selection uses **recorded** order, `(recorded_at,id)`, not `occurred_at`; a backdated observation does not move the row in the list. Default order is `created_at DESC, action_id ASC`, with bounded pages and an opaque cursor bound to tenant, Site, window, selection watermark and last sort key. No unreviewed priority, owner, due date, verification verdict or cross-domain/global Work aggregate is inferred from v6.9.
+
+**Watermark and missingness.** Read one consistent DB snapshot; return `read_at`, max included Action creation/event recorded fence (or explicit zero-row fence), total, page cursor, selected window and `CURRENT` only if the scoped store read succeeds. A zero-row `CURRENT` means no **persisted Actions created in this Site/window**, not no Finding, recommendation, incident, maintenance or unrecorded work. Store unavailable, unauthorized scope or a page cursor whose bound fence changed never becomes an empty list: return a typed failure or invalid-cursor response and reload page one. For selected rows, show the state as of the fence and preserve exact origin/event links after reload. An event arriving after the first-page fence invalidates subsequent pages; do not splice states. Keep exact historical Action detail available separately.
+
+**Refusals and reuse.** Apply `D-2026-10-09-pre-s3-claim-routes-and-actor` identity rules: 401 unauthenticated, 403 known forbidden scope, 404 foreign/nonexistent Site or root. Refuse invalid/unbounded windows and changed cursors; no generic empty success on DB failure. Reuse the Site-scoped Action columns and event ordering in `backend/assetops_backend/actions/adapters/postgres_action_store.py:43-48,184-200`; the Action detail, origin, basis, state and stable links in `backend/assetops_backend/actions/service.py:524-581`; and S6-01 lifecycle/actor authority (`D-2026-10-14-action-lifecycle-and-policy-application`, `D-2026-10-15-s601-action-store-and-ui`, `D-2026-10-15-s601-reopen`). The **new** producer is the Site/window index query, consistent selection/pagination and Site Work UI. The existing `actions_for_finding` is a Finding-scoped helper, not this index. The S4 FindingIndex decision `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index` is the Site/window, cursor and zero-row precedent; it does not select Action rows.
+
+**Fixtures and packaging.** Reuse `D-action-records` for a persisted positive row, adding two Actions at distinct creation times and a later/backdated event to prove order, selected state, exact FindingRevision/event links, same-basis pagination, Site isolation and reload. Add an independently asserted assessed zero-row Site/window and a changed-fence cursor case; a client-filtered empty page is insufficient. S6-03 can be **one S slice**: one small read and one list UI, then independent review and owner checkpoint. It closes only the Actions tab of `sitework:1`, with Incidents/Maintenance and global queues honest gaps.
+
+## S7-01 - `A-S7-health` / `health:2#a` and `asset:3#a`
+
+**Source and scope.** Site Health reads registered gateway/source identities and the most recent centrally stored `GatewayDiagnosticsV1` per gateway for the authenticated Site. Use the server receipt clock for contact freshness under declared cadence, and separately report the diagnostic's own `observed_at`, source contact times, queue depth/oldest age, clock offset/trust, disk pressure and their reported-fact age. Return gateway/source/diagnostic IDs, registration, received/observed clocks, cadence, `read_at`, selected diagnostic sequence and a per-gateway source watermark. A fresh receipt of an old diagnostic makes contact potentially `CURRENT`, while queue/device facts remain old or `UNKNOWN`; virtual Lab time never measures real network lag. Per-source contact is reported only when the diagnostic supplies that source's timestamp; gateway contact alone does not prove a device is connected. `CURRENT | STALE | UNKNOWN` applies to **contact**, not battery condition. `STALE_PROJECTION` is separate if any derived read lags the selected central diagnostic.
+
+The battery asset detail is a separate Site/asset-scoped boundary read. Resolve the configured battery and binding from the effective S1 Site basis, and disclose the selected asset/configuration IDs. In W1, return `UNKNOWN` physical condition and explicit missing causal inputs (for example accepted temperature/capacity/stress history) unless a separately reviewed causal battery observation and rule actually exist. The asset may display accepted SOC or declared nameplate facts only under their own labels and exact accepted evidence/binding IDs; neither proves degradation or remaining life. Withhold replacement exposure, warranty-at-risk, stress score, diagnosis and date. An unbound or absent battery is `NOT_CONFIGURED`/`UNKNOWN` with reason, never a healthy asset or fabricated asset row. `asset:3#b` positive physical condition remains non-core and unmapped.
+
+**Watermark, empty and refusal.** No registered gateway is `NO_REGISTERED_SOURCE`; registered but never received is `UNKNOWN` contact. A stale contact is not proof that an outage began at the last receipt; queue pressure is the last **reported** state, not current hardware pressure. Return a watermarked empty or Unknown with reason, not a green zero. Any unreadable central store, inconsistent registration/diagnostic selection or stale projection refuses a current operational claim. Apply tenant/Site/asset 401/403/404 rules. Do not join diagnostic facts to accepted measurement evidence or claim battery condition from the gateway's own health. Reuse central latest-diagnostic selection in `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/adapters/postgres_gateway_access.py:172-194`, cadence-based contact and diagnostic fields in `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/gateway_access.py:289-343`, and the existing Site gateway read route in `backend/assetops_backend/gateway_api.py:94-109`. The **new** pieces are the selected two-clock fact-age/trust interpretation, bounded Site Health UI and asset Unknown/missing-input read/UI. `D-2026-10-09-pre-s3-evidence-readiness-health` already governs the semantic split; this decision narrows S7's first panel.
+
+**`D-edge-health-cases` and packaging.** Build persisted central diagnostic cases through registered gateway authentication: (1) received/current heartbeat, (2) no later receipt across the declared cadence threshold, (3) delayed old diagnostic received now with old reported facts, (4) growing queue and old queued item, (5) reported clock offset or untrusted clock, and (6) disk pressure/acquisition-stop then recovery. Preserve local sequence, diagnostic ID, server receipt and source reported time; reload the central read. Add never-received and wrong-Site cases plus battery configured-but-causal-input-missing; private Lab state cannot satisfy them. S7-01 needs a **non-UI/UI split** inside its one owner-facing slice: first review diagnostic fixture and two reads, then Health/Asset UI, desktop/narrow behavior, links and owner checkpoint. Do not wait for `I-sim-battery-cases` or a positive physical condition claim.
+
+## S3-02 - `I-capability-read` / `evidence-analysis:2#b`
+
+**Source and scope.** For one authenticated mini-grid Site, selected half-open UTC day and exact selected S3 dispatch window/interval `assessment_revision_id`, derive a versioned CapabilityReadiness/claim ceiling from that assessment's accepted public `EvidenceQuery`/`AnalyticInputSet`, role coverage, quality, cohort, effective Foundation/binding IDs, evidence IDs and S3 rule/limitations. `I-capability-read` returns per-required-role covered duration/quality, missing or conflicted spans, selected accepted IDs, rule and selection basis; `I-claim-limit-read` maps these to what the **selected** dispatch classification can support, including Candidate/Necessary/Indeterminate bounds and named reasons. Do not infer a physical capacity or treat configured capability as observed. The result is assessment-scoped; it cannot silently swap in latest readings or a different day/revision. Reuse `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/dispatch_read.py:123-166` for selected status, basis, coverage, watermarks, window/interval revisions and limitations, and `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/dispatch_read.py:178-202` for exact revision evidence/configuration lineage. The existing Evidence Analysis view in `frontend/src/sites/LedgerSiteEvidence.tsx:93-161,762-775` supplies S2's distinct no-conclusion/status explanation; the **new** read and UI join the S3 selected assessment and express its rule-backed limit.
+
+**Currentness, empty and refusals.** Echo tenant/Site, day/window, `assessment_revision_id`, selected run/model/cohort, knowledge cutoff, selected Foundation/binding IDs, accepted-source and analytic watermarks, `built_at` and current `read_at`. A positive supported ceiling requires a current S3 selection on the exact requested basis, a complete rule-backed assessment of the relevant portion and accepted public role evidence. A current assessment can still have `LIMITED` or `UNKNOWN` portions; name missing-role, poor-quality, conflicting, open-bound, source/cohort or configuration reasons. No selected assessment is `UNASSESSED`, not a zero capability. Pending/failed/dirty or changed S3 selection is `PENDING`/`FAILED`/`STALE_PROJECTION`; show a prior exact revision as historical only and withhold a current positive statement. Reject a foreign Site/revision, a revision outside the selected window, unsupported rule version or absent accepted lineage; preserve the usual 401/403/404 identity boundary. S2's delivered `evidence-analysis:2#a` remains a separate explanation and does not close positive `#b`.
+
+**Fixtures and packaging.** Reuse reviewed `I-sim-dispatch-cases` only after checking exact accepted IDs against this rule: one full Candidate-capability interval, one missing available-PV or PCS-discharge-capability interval, one poor-quality/conflicting interval and one late revision that withdraws currentness. If existing fixtures lack one case, add only that case via registered simulated gateway, ordinary Batch V2 ingestion and S3 assessment; do not use private simulator truth. Assert selected revision and Evidence drill-down after reload, wrong Site/window, stale selection, desktop/narrow and keyboard selection. S3-02 needs a **non-UI/UI split**: review the capability/claim read and fixture first, then the Evidence Analysis UI and owner checkpoint. No broad shared projections or cold-chain/EV role set is required.
+
+## Registry disposition
+
+Resolve only `A-action-index` and `A-S7-health` with the decision anchors below. `I-capability-read` is an implementation node and stays open until reviewed code; `D-edge-health-cases`, all S task nodes and all panel portions stay open until fixture, read, UI and review evidence exist. The three decisions are `D-2026-10-16-a-action-index`, `D-2026-10-16-a-s7-health` and `D-2026-10-16-s302-capability` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`.

+## S6-03 - `A-action-index` / `sitework:1#a`
+
+**Source and scope.** Add a read over persisted `action` and `action_event`, authenticated to one tenant and resolved Site. W1 is mini-grid Actions only. Select a half-open UTC creation window `[from,to)` of at most 31 days; `created_at >= from AND created_at < to` is the membership rule, irrespective of later events. Echo scope and display timezone. The stable row key is `(tenant_id, site_id, action_id)`; include Action code, creation time, exact origin Finding and FindingRevision IDs, latest selected event ID, event recorded time, reduced lifecycle, and the canonical and exact-event Action links. The detail/event selection uses **recorded** order, `(recorded_at,id)`, not `occurred_at`; a backdated observation does not move the row in the list. Default order is `created_at DESC, action_id ASC`, with bounded pages and an opaque cursor bound to tenant, Site, window, selection watermark and last sort key. No unreviewed priority, owner, due date, verification verdict or cross-domain/global Work aggregate is inferred from v6.9.
+
+**Watermark and missingness.** Read one consistent DB snapshot; return `read_at`, max included Action creation/event recorded fence (or explicit zero-row fence), total, page cursor, selected window and `CURRENT` only if the scoped store read succeeds. A zero-row `CURRENT` means no **persisted Actions created in this Site/window**, not no Finding, recommendation, incident, maintenance or unrecorded work. Store unavailable, unauthorized scope or a page cursor whose bound fence changed never becomes an empty list: return a typed failure or invalid-cursor response and reload page one. For selected rows, show the state as of the fence and preserve exact origin/event links after reload. An event arriving after the first-page fence invalidates subsequent pages; do not splice states. Keep exact historical Action detail available separately.
+
+**Refusals and reuse.** Apply `D-2026-10-09-pre-s3-claim-routes-and-actor` identity rules: 401 unauthenticated, 403 known forbidden scope, 404 foreign/nonexistent Site or root. Refuse invalid/unbounded windows and changed cursors; no generic empty success on DB failure. Reuse the Site-scoped Action columns and event ordering in `backend/assetops_backend/actions/adapters/postgres_action_store.py:43-48,184-200`; the Action detail, origin, basis, state and stable links in `backend/assetops_backend/actions/service.py:524-581`; and S6-01 lifecycle/actor authority (`D-2026-10-14-action-lifecycle-and-policy-application`, `D-2026-10-15-s601-action-store-and-ui`, `D-2026-10-15-s601-reopen`). The **new** producer is the Site/window index query, consistent selection/pagination and Site Work UI. The existing `actions_for_finding` is a Finding-scoped helper, not this index. The S4 FindingIndex decision `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index` is the Site/window, cursor and zero-row precedent; it does not select Action rows.
+
+**Fixtures and packaging.** Reuse `D-action-records` for a persisted positive row, adding two Actions at distinct creation times and a later/backdated event to prove order, selected state, exact FindingRevision/event links, same-basis pagination, Site isolation and reload. Add an independently asserted assessed zero-row Site/window and a changed-fence cursor case; a client-filtered empty page is insufficient. S6-03 can be **one S slice**: one small read and one list UI, then independent review and owner checkpoint. It closes only the Actions tab of `sitework:1`, with Incidents/Maintenance and global queues honest gaps.
+
+## S7-01 - `A-S7-health` / `health:2#a` and `asset:3#a`
+
+**Source and scope.** Site Health reads registered gateway/source identities and the most recent centrally stored `GatewayDiagnosticsV1` per gateway for the authenticated Site. Use the server receipt clock for contact freshness under declared cadence, and separately report the diagnostic's own `observed_at`, source contact times, queue depth/oldest age, clock offset/trust, disk pressure and their reported-fact age. Return gateway/source/diagnostic IDs, registration, received/observed clocks, cadence, `read_at`, selected diagnostic sequence and a per-gateway source watermark. A fresh receipt of an old diagnostic makes contact potentially `CURRENT`, while queue/device facts remain old or `UNKNOWN`; virtual Lab time never measures real network lag. Per-source contact is reported only when the diagnostic supplies that source's timestamp; gateway contact alone does not prove a device is connected. `CURRENT | STALE | UNKNOWN` applies to **contact**, not battery condition. `STALE_PROJECTION` is separate if any derived read lags the selected central diagnostic.
+
+The battery asset detail is a separate Site/asset-scoped boundary read. Resolve the configured battery and binding from the effective S1 Site basis, and disclose the selected asset/configuration IDs. In W1, return `UNKNOWN` physical condition and explicit missing causal inputs (for example accepted temperature/capacity/stress history) unless a separately reviewed causal battery observation and rule actually exist. The asset may display accepted SOC or declared nameplate facts only under their own labels and exact accepted evidence/binding IDs; neither proves degradation or remaining life. Withhold replacement exposure, warranty-at-risk, stress score, diagnosis and date. An unbound or absent battery is `NOT_CONFIGURED`/`UNKNOWN` with reason, never a healthy asset or fabricated asset row. `asset:3#b` positive physical condition remains non-core and unmapped.
+
+**Watermark, empty and refusal.** No registered gateway is `NO_REGISTERED_SOURCE`; registered but never received is `UNKNOWN` contact. A stale contact is not proof that an outage began at the last receipt; queue pressure is the last **reported** state, not current hardware pressure. Return a watermarked empty or Unknown with reason, not a green zero. Any unreadable central store, inconsistent registration/diagnostic selection or stale projection refuses a current operational claim. Apply tenant/Site/asset 401/403/404 rules. Do not join diagnostic facts to accepted measurement evidence or claim battery condition from the gateway's own health. Reuse central latest-diagnostic selection in `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/adapters/postgres_gateway_access.py:172-194`, cadence-based contact and diagnostic fields in `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/gateway_access.py:289-343`, and the existing Site gateway read route in `backend/assetops_backend/gateway_api.py:94-109`. The **new** pieces are the selected two-clock fact-age/trust interpretation, bounded Site Health UI and asset Unknown/missing-input read/UI. `D-2026-10-09-pre-s3-evidence-readiness-health` already governs the semantic split; this decision narrows S7's first panel.
+
+**`D-edge-health-cases` and packaging.** Build persisted central diagnostic cases through registered gateway authentication: (1) received/current heartbeat, (2) no later receipt across the declared cadence threshold, (3) delayed old diagnostic received now with old reported facts, (4) growing queue and old queued item, (5) reported clock offset or untrusted clock, and (6) disk pressure/acquisition-stop then recovery. Preserve local sequence, diagnostic ID, server receipt and source reported time; reload the central read. Add never-received and wrong-Site cases plus battery configured-but-causal-input-missing; private Lab state cannot satisfy them. S7-01 needs a **non-UI/UI split** inside its one owner-facing slice: first review diagnostic fixture and two reads, then Health/Asset UI, desktop/narrow behavior, links and owner checkpoint. Do not wait for `I-sim-battery-cases` or a positive physical condition claim.
+
+## S3-02 - `I-capability-read` / `evidence-analysis:2#b`
+
+**Source and scope.** For one authenticated mini-grid Site, selected half-open UTC day and exact selected S3 dispatch window/interval `assessment_revision_id`, derive a versioned CapabilityReadiness/claim ceiling from that assessment's accepted public `EvidenceQuery`/`AnalyticInputSet`, role coverage, quality, cohort, effective Foundation/binding IDs, evidence IDs and S3 rule/limitations. `I-capability-read` returns per-required-role covered duration/quality, missing or conflicted spans, selected accepted IDs, rule and selection basis; `I-claim-limit-read` maps these to what the **selected** dispatch classification can support, including Candidate/Necessary/Indeterminate bounds and named reasons. Do not infer a physical capacity or treat configured capability as observed. The result is assessment-scoped; it cannot silently swap in latest readings or a different day/revision. Reuse `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/dispatch_read.py:123-166` for selected status, basis, coverage, watermarks, window/interval revisions and limitations, and `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/dispatch_read.py:178-202` for exact revision evidence/configuration lineage. The existing Evidence Analysis view in `frontend/src/sites/LedgerSiteEvidence.tsx:93-161,762-775` supplies S2's distinct no-conclusion/status explanation; the **new** read and UI join the S3 selected assessment and express its rule-backed limit.
+
+**Currentness, empty and refusals.** Echo tenant/Site, day/window, `assessment_revision_id`, selected run/model/cohort, knowledge cutoff, selected Foundation/binding IDs, accepted-source and analytic watermarks, `built_at` and current `read_at`. A positive supported ceiling requires a current S3 selection on the exact requested basis, a complete rule-backed assessment of the relevant portion and accepted public role evidence. A current assessment can still have `LIMITED` or `UNKNOWN` portions; name missing-role, poor-quality, conflicting, open-bound, source/cohort or configuration reasons. No selected assessment is `UNASSESSED`, not a zero capability. Pending/failed/dirty or changed S3 selection is `PENDING`/`FAILED`/`STALE_PROJECTION`; show a prior exact revision as historical only and withhold a current positive statement. Reject a foreign Site/revision, a revision outside the selected window, unsupported rule version or absent accepted lineage; preserve the usual 401/403/404 identity boundary. S2's delivered `evidence-analysis:2#a` remains a separate explanation and does not close positive `#b`.
+
+**Fixtures and packaging.** Reuse reviewed `I-sim-dispatch-cases` only after checking exact accepted IDs against this rule: one full Candidate-capability interval, one missing available-PV or PCS-discharge-capability interval, one poor-quality/conflicting interval and one late revision that withdraws currentness. If existing fixtures lack one case, add only that case via registered simulated gateway, ordinary Batch V2 ingestion and S3 assessment; do not use private simulator truth. Assert selected revision and Evidence drill-down after reload, wrong Site/window, stale selection, desktop/narrow and keyboard selection. S3-02 needs a **non-UI/UI split**: review the capability/claim read and fixture first, then the Evidence Analysis UI and owner checkpoint. No broad shared projections or cold-chain/EV role set is required.
+
+## Registry disposition
+
+Resolve only `A-action-index` and `A-S7-health` with the decision anchors below. `I-capability-read` is an implementation node and stays open until reviewed code; `D-edge-health-cases`, all S task nodes and all panel portions stay open until fixture, read, UI and review evidence exist. The three decisions are `D-2026-10-16-a-action-index`, `D-2026-10-16-a-s7-health` and `D-2026-10-16-s302-capability` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`.

Amend S5-02 question 1: assert parity between the S5-01 selected Finding and S4-04 first index row only for the same imported Site and half-open UTC day when the index is current, the Overview choice is freshly discovered under an open gate, comparable selected-day S3/S4 watermark fields agree, and both Finding and exact revision IDs match. The index's full continuity-day token and the Overview's accepted-source fingerprint have different scopes and are not equality keys. Otherwise display each read's basis and offer reload without a parity claim; persistent ID disagreement on settled comparable bases is a contract defect. Keep the reads separate and do not replace either choice. Required S5-02 packet and proof changes: `.agent/architect-S5-02-answers.md`.
+
+Decision: `D-2026-10-16-a-action-index`. Resolve `A-action-index` for the W1 Site Work Actions list only. Select persisted Actions created in an authenticated tenant/Site half-open UTC window of at most 31 days. Stable identity is `(tenant_id,site_id,action_id)`; order by `created_at` descending then Action UUID ascending, and bind bounded cursors to the consistent row/event selection fence. Return exact origin FindingRevision, latest selected ActionEvent and canonical/exact-event links. A watermarked zero means no persisted Actions created in this scope, not no work of another kind; changed cursors, store failure and unauthorized scope cannot produce empty success. Backdated `occurred_at` does not change recorded event order or list membership. S6-03 adds the index read and Site UI as one reviewed slice with separate persisted positive and zero cases, leaving Incidents, Maintenance and global/cross-domain Work out of scope. Full contract: `.agent/architect-W1-remaining-answers.md`.
+
+Decision: `D-2026-10-16-a-s7-health`. Resolve `A-S7-health` for W1 `health:2#a` and the Unknown-only `asset:3#a`. Site Health selects centrally received gateway diagnostics and registered cadence per authenticated Site; server-receipt contact freshness is separate from the age/trust of gateway-reported queue, clock, disk and source-contact facts. A newly received old diagnostic can show current gateway contact and old/Unknown facts. No gateway, no receipt, stale receipt, and stale projection are distinct watermarked states. Site Health makes no equipment-condition claim. The configured battery detail returns physical condition Unknown with named missing causal inputs; SOC or declared rating alone cannot yield degradation, remaining life, warranty or replacement exposure. Positive `asset:3#b` remains unmapped. `D-edge-health-cases` must persist central heartbeat, stale/no contact, delayed old fact, queue growth, clock offset and disk-pressure/recovery cases, with exact diagnostic IDs, server and reported times, reload, never-received and wrong-Site proofs. S7-01 uses a non-UI/read-and-fixture phase followed by UI and owner review. Full contract: `.agent/architect-W1-remaining-answers.md`; it narrows `D-2026-10-09-pre-s3-evidence-readiness-health` without promoting a panel.
+
+Decision: `D-2026-10-16-s302-capability`. `I-capability-read` is the bounded implementation of `D-2026-10-09-pre-s3-evidence-readiness-health` for one exact selected S3 mini-grid dispatch assessment and accepted public role coverage. S3-02 binds Site, UTC day/window, assessment revision, run/model/cohort, knowledge cutoff, effective Foundation/bindings, accepted evidence IDs and source/analytic watermarks; `I-claim-limit-read` maps that assessment's versioned rule and limitations to a supported classification ceiling with missing/quality/conflict reasons. Pending, stale, failed or unassessed selection cannot produce a current positive claim; an old exact revision may be shown only as historical. Do not substitute configured capability, gateway contact or private Lab truth. The positive `evidence-analysis:2#b` requires rule-backed complete and missing fixtures, exact Evidence drill-down, a reviewed non-UI read phase and then UI/owner review; S2's `#a` explanation stays distinct. This decision does not resolve the implementation node or panel. Full contract: `.agent/architect-W1-remaining-answers.md`.
